## Effect

| Token Name | CSS Variable | Value | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `effect/Focus Ring/Default` | `--effect-focus-ring-default` | `0px 0px 0px 3px rgb(212, 212, 212)` | Gray focus ring (3 px spread) — used as the default/neutral focus indicator. |
| `effect/Focus Ring/Error` | `--effect-focus-ring-error` | `0px 0px 0px 3px rgb(253, 164, 175)` | Pink focus ring (3 px spread) — applied to focused elements in an error state. |
| `effect/Focus Ring/Primary` | `--effect-focus-ring-primary` | `0px 0px 0px 3px rgb(255, 176, 136)` | Orange focus ring (3 px spread) — applied to elements in an active focus state. |
| `effect/Shadow/2xl` | `--effect-shadow-2xl` | `0px 25px 50px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25)` | Largest drop shadow — y:25, blur:50, spread:12, 25% opacity. Use for elements with the highest elevation. |
| `effect/Shadow/2xs` | `--effect-shadow-2xs` | `0px 1px 0px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05)` | Lightest drop shadow — y:1, blur:0, 5% opacity. Use for subtle dividers between elements. |
| `effect/Shadow/lg` | `--effect-shadow-lg` | `0px 10px 15px -3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1), 0px 4px 6px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1)` | Large drop shadow — two layers (y:10 blur:15 + y:4 blur:6), 10% opacity. Use for modals and dialogs. |
| `effect/Shadow/md` | `--effect-shadow-md` | `0px 4px 6px -1px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1), 0px 2px 4px -2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1)` | Medium drop shadow — two layers (y:4 blur:6 + y:2 blur:4), 10% opacity. Use for dropdowns and popovers. |
| `effect/Shadow/sm` | `--effect-shadow-sm` | `0px 1px 3px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1), 0px 1px 2px -1px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1)` | Small drop shadow — two layers (y:1 blur:3 + y:1 blur:2), 10% opacity. Use for cards and interactive elements. |
| `effect/Shadow/xl` | `--effect-shadow-xl` | `0px 20px 25px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1), 0px 8px 10px -6px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1)` | Extra-large drop shadow — two layers (y:20 blur:25 + y:8 blur:10), 10% opacity. Use for primary floating elements. |
| `effect/Shadow/xs` | `--effect-shadow-xs` | `0px 1px 2px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05)` | Extra-small drop shadow — y:1, blur:2, 5% opacity. Use for minimal elevation on cards or buttons. |

| Token Name | CSS Variable | Value | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `typography/Body/Bold` | `--typography-body-bold` | `14px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Plus Jakarta Sans SemiBold 14/21. Bold paragraph text for strong emphasis within content. |
| `typography/Body/Medium` | `--typography-body-medium` | `14px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Plus Jakarta Sans Medium 14/21. Medium-weight paragraph text for subtle emphasis within content. |
| `typography/Body/Regular` | `--typography-body-regular` | `14px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Plus Jakarta Sans Regular 14/21. Default paragraph text for general content. |
| `typography/Caption/Bold` | `--typography-caption-bold` | `12px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Plus Jakarta Sans SemiBold 12/16. Bold small text for emphasized captions or annotations. |
| `typography/Caption/Medium` | `--typography-caption-medium` | `12px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Plus Jakarta Sans Medium 12/16. Medium-weight small text for badge labels or tags. |
| `typography/Caption/Regular` | `--typography-caption-regular` | `12px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Plus Jakarta Sans Regular 12/16. Small text for captions, helper text, or metadata. |
| `typography/Heading 1` | `--typography-heading-1` | `48px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Plus Jakarta Sans SemiBold 48/48, letter-spacing -1.5 px. Use for primary page titles and hero headings. |
| `typography/Heading 2` | `--typography-heading-2` | `30px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Plus Jakarta Sans SemiBold 30/30, letter-spacing -1 px. Use for major section headings. |
| `typography/Heading 3` | `--typography-heading-3` | `24px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Plus Jakarta Sans SemiBold 24/28.8, letter-spacing -1 px. Use for sub-section headings or card titles. |
| `typography/Heading 4` | `--typography-heading-4` | `20px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Plus Jakarta Sans SemiBold 20/24. Use for component headings or small section titles. |
| `typography/Label/Bold` | `--typography-label-bold` | `11px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Typography sourced from Semantic variables. |
| `typography/Label/Medium` | `--typography-label-medium` | `11px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Typography sourced from Semantic variables. |
| `typography/Label/Regular` | `--typography-label-regular` | `11px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Label text style for compact component labels. Typography is sourced from Semantic variables. |
| `typography/Title/Bold` | `--typography-title-bold` | `16px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Plus Jakarta Sans SemiBold 16/24. Bold title text for labels that need strong emphasis. |
| `typography/Title/Medium` | `--typography-title-medium` | `16px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Plus Jakarta Sans Medium 16/24. Medium-weight title text for labels that need slight emphasis. |
| `typography/Title/Regular` | `--typography-title-regular` | `16px "Plus Jakarta Sans"` | Plus Jakarta Sans Regular 16/24. Normal-weight title text for labels or lightweight sub-headings. |
